+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset, const int width) {
+    const int rowIndex = offset + width + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    for (int column = 0; column < width; ++column) {
+        const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < offset + column; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+        }
+        matrix[row + offset + column] =
+            (matrix[row + offset + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + offset + column];
+    }
+}
+
+// Lower-triangular SYRK update. Each output element is independent and is
+// assigned to one CUDA thread; the dependency between panels is enforced by
+// the host-side stream ordering.
+__global__ void updateTrailingMatrix(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
+                                     const int width, const int end) {
+    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
+    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;
+
+    if (rowIndex < n && columnIndex < n && rowIndex >= columnIndex) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + offset + k] *
+                   matrix[static_cast<size_t>(columnIndex) * n + offset + k];
+        }
+        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + columnIndex] -= sum;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+

+// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
+// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.

+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

+    }

+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);

+    }

+
+// Lower-triangular SYRK update. Each output element is independent and is
+// assigned to one CUDA thread; the dependency between panels is enforced by
+// the host-side stream ordering.
+__global__ void updateTrailingMatrix(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
+                                     const int width, const int end) {
+    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
+    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;
+
+    if (rowIndex < n && columnIndex < n && rowIndex >= columnIndex) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + offset + k] *
+                   matrix[static_cast<size_t>(columnIndex) * n + offset + k];
+        }
+        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + columnIndex] -= sum;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
+// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
+    bool success = false;
+    bool executionError = false;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
+        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = 0; offset < dimension; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = std::min(kBlockSize, dimension - offset);
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, kBlockSize>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, offset, width,
+                                               devicePositiveDefinite);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
+            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization") ) {
+            executionError = true;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        int positiveDefinite = 1;
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
+            positiveDefinite == 0) {
+            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", offset);
+            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
+            break;
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        const int rowsBelow = dimension - (offset + width);
+        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
+            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
+            solvePanel<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, offset, width);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
+
+            const dim3 threads(kBlockSize, kBlockSize);
+            const dim3 grid((rowsBelow + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize,
+                            (rowsBelow + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+            updateTrailingMatrix<<<grid, threads>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, offset, width,
+                                                     offset + width);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "trailing update")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
         }
+        success = true;
+    }
+
+    if (success && !executionError) {
+        const size_t elements = n * n;
+        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
+        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                         "matrix download");
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+    return success;
 }

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset, const int width) {
+    const int rowIndex = offset + width + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {

+        matrix[row + offset + column] =

+        }

+}

+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;

+    }
+}

+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
+// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
+    bool success = false;
+    bool executionError = false;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
+        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = 0; offset < dimension; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = std::min(kBlockSize, dimension - offset);
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, kBlockSize>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, offset, width,
+                                               devicePositiveDefinite);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
+            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization") ) {
+            executionError = true;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        int positiveDefinite = 1;
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
+            positiveDefinite == 0) {
+            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", offset);
+            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
+            break;
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        const int rowsBelow = dimension - (offset + width);
+        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
+            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
+            solvePanel<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, offset, width);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
+
+            const dim3 threads(kBlockSize, kBlockSize);
+            const dim3 grid((rowsBelow + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize,
+                            (rowsBelow + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+            updateTrailingMatrix<<<grid, threads>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, offset, width,
+                                                     offset + width);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "trailing update")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
         }
+        success = true;
+    }
+
+    if (success && !executionError) {
+        const size_t elements = n * n;
+        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
+        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                         "matrix download");

-    
-    return true;

+    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+    return success;

codex
The second test shows the shared-memory change was not the root cause; the failure is in the blocked panel/update dependency path for multi-tile trailing regions. To guarantee equivalent numerical semantics, I’m switching the launch path to a GPU-parallel column algorithm that mirrors the original recurrence exactly: one diagonal kernel per column and one parallel kernel for all entries below that diagonal. This retains CUDA parallelism and correctness while avoiding any altered blocked-update ordering.
apply patch

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset, const int width) {
+    const int rowIndex = offset + width + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    for (int column = 0; column < width; ++column) {
+        const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < offset + column; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+        }
+        matrix[row + offset + column] =
+            (matrix[row + offset + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + offset + column];
+    }
+}
+
+// Lower-triangular SYRK update. Each output element is independent and is
+// assigned to one CUDA thread; the dependency between panels is enforced by
+// the host-side stream ordering.
+__global__ void updateTrailingMatrix(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
+                                     const int width, const int end) {
+    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
+    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;
+
+    if (rowIndex < n && columnIndex < n && rowIndex >= columnIndex) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + offset + k] *
+                   matrix[static_cast<size_t>(columnIndex) * n + offset + k];
+        }
+        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + columnIndex] -= sum;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
+                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
+    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;

+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
+// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
+    bool success = false;
+    bool executionError = false;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
+        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    for (int column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
+        factorColumnDiagonal<<<1, 1>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column,
+                                       devicePositiveDefinite);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
+            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization")) {
+            executionError = true;
+            break;
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        int positiveDefinite = 1;
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
+            positiveDefinite == 0) {
+            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", column);
+            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        const int rowsBelow = dimension - column - 1;
+        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
+            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
+            factorColumnBelow<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
         }
+        success = true;
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    if (success && !executionError) {
+        const size_t elements = n * n;
+        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
+        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                         "matrix download");
+    }
+
+    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+    return success;
 }

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset, const int width) {
+    const int rowIndex = offset + width + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    for (int column = 0; column < width; ++column) {
+        const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < offset + column; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+        }
+        matrix[row + offset + column] =
+            (matrix[row + offset + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + offset + column];
+    }
+}
+
+// Lower-triangular SYRK update. Each output element is independent and is
+// assigned to one CUDA thread; the dependency between panels is enforced by
+// the host-side stream ordering.
+__global__ void updateTrailingMatrix(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
+                                     const int width, const int end) {
+    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
+    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;
+
+    if (rowIndex < n && columnIndex < n && rowIndex >= columnIndex) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + offset + k] *
+                   matrix[static_cast<size_t>(columnIndex) * n + offset + k];
+        }
+        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + columnIndex] -= sum;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
+                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
+    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset, const int width) {
+    const int rowIndex = offset + width + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    for (int column = 0; column < width; ++column) {
+        const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < offset + column; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+        }
+        matrix[row + offset + column] =
+            (matrix[row + offset + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + offset + column];
+    }
+}
+
+// Lower-triangular SYRK update. Each output element is independent and is
+// assigned to one CUDA thread; the dependency between panels is enforced by
+// the host-side stream ordering.
+__global__ void updateTrailingMatrix(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
+                                     const int width, const int end) {
+    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
+    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;
+
+    if (rowIndex < n && columnIndex < n && rowIndex >= columnIndex) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + offset + k] *
+                   matrix[static_cast<size_t>(columnIndex) * n + offset + k];
+        }
+        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + columnIndex] -= sum;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
+                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
+    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
+// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
+    bool success = false;
+    bool executionError = false;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
+        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    for (int column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
+        factorColumnDiagonal<<<1, 1>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column,
+                                       devicePositiveDefinite);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
+            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization")) {
+            executionError = true;
+            break;
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        int positiveDefinite = 1;
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
+            positiveDefinite == 0) {
+            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", column);
+            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        const int rowsBelow = dimension - column - 1;
+        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
+            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
+            factorColumnBelow<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
         }
+        success = true;
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    if (success && !executionError) {
+        const size_t elements = n * n;
+        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
+        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                         "matrix download");
+    }
+
+    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+    return success;
 }

    16	constexpr int kBlockSize = 32;
    17	
    18	__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
    19	                                    const int width, int* positiveDefinite) {
    20	    __shared__ double diagonal[kBlockSize];
    21	    const int thread = threadIdx.x;
    22	
    23	    for (int column = 0; column < width; ++column) {
    24	        if (thread == column) {
    25	            const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
    26	            double sum = 0.0;
    27	            for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
    28	                const double value = matrix[row + offset + k];
    29	                sum += value * value;
    30	            }
    31	            const double value = matrix[row + offset + column] - sum;
    32	            if (value <= 0.0) {
    33	                *positiveDefinite = 0;
    34	            } else {
    35	                diagonal[column] = sqrt(value);
    36	                matrix[row + offset + column] = diagonal[column];
    37	            }
    38	        }
    39	        __syncthreads();
    40	
    41	        if (*positiveDefinite == 0) {
    42	            return;
    43	        }
    44	        if (thread > column && thread < width) {
    45	            const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(offset + thread) * n;
    46	            const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
    47	            double sum = 0.0;
    48	            for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
    49	                sum += matrix[row + offset + k] * matrix[pivotRow + offset + k];
    50	            }
    51	            matrix[row + offset + column] =
    52	                (matrix[row + offset + column] - sum) / diagonal[column];
    53	        }
    54	        __syncthreads();
    55	    }
    56	}

    81	    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
    82	    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;

   144	// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
   145	// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
   146	// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.
   147	
   148	bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   149	    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   150	        return false;
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
   154	    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
   155	    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
   156	    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
   157	    bool success = false;
   158	    bool executionError = false;
   159	
   160	    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
   161	        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
   162	        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   163	                "matrix upload") ||
   164	        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
   165	        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
   166	        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
   167	        return false;
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    for (int column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
   171	        factorColumnDiagonal<<<1, 1>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column,
   172	                                       devicePositiveDefinite);
   173	        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
   174	            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization")) {
   175	            executionError = true;
   176	            break;
   177	        }
   178	
   179	        int positiveDefinite = 1;
   180	        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
   181	                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
   182	            positiveDefinite == 0) {
   183	            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", column);
   184	            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
   185	            break;
   186	        }
   187	
   188	        const int rowsBelow = dimension - column - 1;
   189	        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
   190	            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
   191	            factorColumnBelow<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column);
   192	            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
   193	                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
   194	                executionError = true;
   195	                break;
   196	            }
   197	        }
   198	        success = true;
   199	    }
   200	
   201	    if (success && !executionError) {
   202	        const size_t elements = n * n;
   203	        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
   204	        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
   205	                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
   206	                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   207	                         "matrix download");
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
   211	    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
   212	    return success;
   213	}
   214	
   215	// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
   216	void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   217	    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
   218	    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
   219	    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
   220	    
   221	    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
   222	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   223	    
   224	    // Generate random matrix B
   225	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
   226	        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
   227	    }
   228	    
   229	    // Compute A = B * B^T
   230	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   230	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   231	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   232	            double sum = 0.0;
   233	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
   234	                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
   235	            }
   236	            A[i * n + j] = sum;
   237	        }
   238	    }
   239	    
   240	    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
   241	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   242	        A[i * n + i] += n;
   243	    }
   244	}
   245	
   246	bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
   247	    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
   248	    
   249	    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
   250	    
   251	    // Compute L * L^T
   252	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   253	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   254	            double sum = 0.0;
   255	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
   256	                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
   257	            }
   258	            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
   259	        }
   260	    }
   261	    
   262	    // Compare with original
   263	    double maxError = 0.0;
   264	    double relError = 0.0;
   265	    size_t maxErrorIndex = 0;
   266	    
   267	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
   268	        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
   269	        if (error > maxError) {
   270	            maxError = error;
   271	            maxErrorIndex = i;
   272	        }
   273	        
   274	        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
   275	        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
   276	    }
   277	    
   278	    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
   279	    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
   280	    printf("Max error index: (%zu, %zu)\n", maxErrorIndex / n, maxErrorIndex % n);
   281	    
   282	    // Check if error is within tolerance
   283	    if (relError > 1e-6) {
   284	        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
   285	        return false;
   286	    }
   287	    
   288	    return true;
   289	}

   300	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   301	    size_t n = 512;
   302	    bool validate = false;
   303	    bool printResults = false;
   304	    
   305	    // Parse command line arguments

   316	        } else {
   317	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   318	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   319	            return 1;

   321	    }
   322	    
   323	    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");

   325	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   326

   337	    }
   338	    
   339	    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
   340	    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
   341	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   342	    
   343	    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
   344	    
   345	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   346	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   347	    
   348	    if (!success) {

   350	        return 1;

   353	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());

   355	    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
   356	    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
   357	    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
   358	    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   359	    
   360	    // Print results for external validation

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset, const int width) {
+    const int rowIndex = offset + width + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    for (int column = 0; column < width; ++column) {
+        const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(offset + column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < offset + column; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+        }
+        matrix[row + offset + column] =
+            (matrix[row + offset + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + offset + column];
+    }
+}
+
+// Lower-triangular SYRK update. Each output element is independent and is
+// assigned to one CUDA thread; the dependency between panels is enforced by
+// the host-side stream ordering.
+__global__ void updateTrailingMatrix(double* matrix, const int n, const int offset,
+                                     const int width, const int end) {
+    const int rowIndex = end + blockIdx.y * kBlockSize + threadIdx.y;
+    const int columnIndex = end + blockIdx.x * kBlockSize + threadIdx.x;
+
+    if (rowIndex < n && columnIndex < n && rowIndex >= columnIndex) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < width; ++k) {
+            sum += matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + offset + k] *
+                   matrix[static_cast<size_t>(columnIndex) * n + offset + k];
+        }
+        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n + columnIndex] -= sum;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
+                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
+    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Blocked right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  All matrix arithmetic is
+// performed on the GPU; the host only launches the dependency-ordered panels.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
+    bool success = false;
+    bool executionError = false;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
+        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    for (int column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
+        factorColumnDiagonal<<<1, 1>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column,
+                                       devicePositiveDefinite);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
+            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization")) {
+            executionError = true;
+            break;
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        int positiveDefinite = 1;
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
+            positiveDefinite == 0) {
+            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", column);
+            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        const int rowsBelow = dimension - column - 1;
+        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
+            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
+            factorColumnBelow<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
         }
+        success = true;
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    if (success && !executionError) {
+        const size_t elements = n * n;
+        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
+        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                         "matrix download");
+    }
+
+    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+    return success;
 }

+namespace {
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
+                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
+    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
+

+// triangular update is distributed across CUDA threads while the sequential
+// column dependency is preserved exactly.
+// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.

cholesky.cpp
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

namespace {

__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
        matrix[index] = 0.0;
    }
}

__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
            const double value = matrix[row + k];
            sum += value * value;
        }
        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
        if (value <= 0.0) {
            *positiveDefinite = 0;
        } else {
            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
        }
    }
}

__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (rowIndex >= n) {
        return;
    }
    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
    double sum = 0.0;
    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
    }
    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
}

bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
        return true;
    }
    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
    return false;
}

} // namespace

// GPU-parallel column-oriented Cholesky decomposition. Every column's lower
// triangular update is distributed across CUDA threads while the sequential
// column dependency is preserved exactly.
// A is stored in row-major order and overwritten with the lower factor.

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    if (n == 0 || n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
        return false;
    }

    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(n);
    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
    bool success = false;
    bool executionError = false;

    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
                "matrix upload") ||
        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
        return false;
    }

    for (int column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
        factorColumnDiagonal<<<1, 1>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column,
                                       devicePositiveDefinite);
        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization")) {
            executionError = true;
            break;
        }

        int positiveDefinite = 1;
        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
            positiveDefinite == 0) {
            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", column);
            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
            break;
        }

        const int rowsBelow = dimension - column - 1;
        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
            factorColumnBelow<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column);
            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
                executionError = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        success = true;
    }

    if (success && !executionError) {
        const size_t elements = n * n;
        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
                         "matrix download");
    }

    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
    return success;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }

+    int* devicePositiveDefinite = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = n * n * sizeof(double);
+    bool success = false;
+    bool executionError = false;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceMatrix, bytes), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceMatrix, A.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(devicePositiveDefinite, 1, sizeof(int)), "status initialization")) {
+        cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    for (int column = 0; column < dimension; ++column) {
+        factorColumnDiagonal<<<1, 1>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column,
+                                       devicePositiveDefinite);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal factorization launch") ||
+            !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "diagonal factorization")) {
+            executionError = true;
+            break;
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        int positiveDefinite = 1;
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&positiveDefinite, devicePositiveDefinite, sizeof(int),
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download") ||
+            positiveDefinite == 0) {
+            printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", column);
+            executionError = positiveDefinite != 0;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        const int rowsBelow = dimension - column - 1;
+        if (rowsBelow > 0) {
+            const int blocks = (rowsBelow + 255) / 256;
+            factorColumnBelow<<<blocks, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension, column);
+            if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve launch") ||
+                !cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "panel solve")) {
+                executionError = true;
+                break;
+            }
         }
+        success = true;
     }
-    
-    return true;
+
+    if (success && !executionError) {
+        const size_t elements = n * n;
+        clearUpperTriangle<<<(elements + 255) / 256, 256>>>(deviceMatrix, dimension);
+        success = cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup launch") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "upper triangle cleanup") &&
+                  cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), deviceMatrix, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                         "matrix download");
+    }
+
+    cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+    cudaFree(devicePositiveDefinite);
+    return success;
 }

 #include <cstdio>
 #include <cstdlib>
 #include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+__global__ void clearUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const int n) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    if (index < total && index % n > index / n) {
+        matrix[index] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnDiagonal(double* matrix, const int n, const int column,
+                                     int* positiveDefinite) {
+    if (blockIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+            const double value = matrix[row + k];
+            sum += value * value;
+        }
+        const double value = matrix[row + column] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0) {
+            *positiveDefinite = 0;
+        } else {
+            matrix[row + column] = sqrt(value);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void factorColumnBelow(double* matrix, const int n, const int column) {
+    const int rowIndex = column + 1 + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (rowIndex >= n) {
+        return;
+    }
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(rowIndex) * n;
+    const size_t pivotRow = static_cast<size_t>(column) * n;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int k = 0; k < column; ++k) {
+        sum += matrix[row + k] * matrix[pivotRow + k];
+    }
+    matrix[row + column] = (matrix[row + column] - sum) / matrix[pivotRow + column];
+}
+
+bool cudaOk(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace
 
+// GPU-parallel column-oriented Cholesky decomposition. Every column's lower
+// triangular update is distributed across CUDA threads while the sequential
+// column dependency is preserved exactly.
